# Compatibility report: 3.6.0-20261113.210402-2 against 3.5.2

## What this report does NOT cover

Silence is not compatibility: nothing below has a cell in this report.

- Write paths: the JSONB write executor (create, replace, merge patch, JSON patch, delete), the version resolver, the split sub-resource controllers and child counter.
- Paging and the response: offset/limit bounds, the Link and count headers, fields= selection (@Tmf630Response), the undeclared query-parameter guard.
- Settings other than the defaults the suite runs with: isnull-semantics NULLISH, regex disabled, allowlists, on-unknown-* policies other than the defaults, combine modes, implicit-eq variants, the TMF-630 §4.4 encoded operator forms.
- JSONB sorts on a split collection and through an array of arrays; correlated sorts (sort=arr[key=X].value) and the sort-key validation.
- Mongo: sorts and the aggregation module; split collections; dates and date-times stored as BSON dates (the twin holds the suite's JSON, where they are strings).
- JPA: nested and array positions beyond the ledger's joined-collection samples; the Object, Map and list kinds (a typed column cannot hold them); positional [N]; sorts.
- Backends other than PostgreSQL 18.1 and Mongo 8.0.5 (other PostgreSQL versions, H2, other JPA databases).

## The same rows in both versions, but not the oracle's (described, not a gate)

Defects of the release, not of the candidate, unless a documented reason is shown. The oracle is the suite's (ComboOracle) over the documents each backend holds; it reads the JSON as JSONB stores it. The suite's pins are those of expected-defaults.tsv as committed.

- JPA: filter= != does not match a missing or null value (README, "What ne, nin and != mean on each backend"): 9
- Mongo: a scalar or object stored where a list is declared matches as one element (Mongo's query semantics; the twin holds the suite's JSON as written): 120
- Mongo: ne / nin / != (README, "What ne, nin and != mean on each backend"): 100
- Mongo: the twin holds dates as strings (not covered): 62
- oracle limit: its Jayway leaf compares a list as a whole; the backends match a list leaf by element (jsonpath lax mode, Mongo arrays): 54
- oracle limit: its Jayway leaf compares date-times as text; JPA compares instants (2025-01-01T10:00:00+02:00 equals 2025-01-01T08:00:00Z): 1
- oracle limit: its Jayway leaf does not order booleans; the backends order false < true: 32
- oracle limit: its Jayway leaf finds a stored "5" for == 5; the backends are type-exact there (the suite pins the same meaning on JSONB): 8
- pinned in the suite: ACCEPTANCE: type check not shipped; held (TOOLKIT-JSONB-TYPE-GATE-AS-QUERYDSL-1): JSONB answers what the QueryDSL terminal refuses: 115
- pinned in the suite: ROWS: HELD TOOLKIT-JSONB-TYPE-EXACT-EVERYWHERE-RULING-1: a JSON number in a date field (20250101) is read as a date through its text; equality and ranges agree here (dates keep the extraction since #16), both read the number; under type-exact the date guard gains jsonb_typeof = string: 3
- pinned in the suite: ROWS: HELD TOOLKIT-JSONB-TYPE-EXACT-EVERYWHERE-RULING-1: a value operator other than the containment equality reads a value of another JSON type through its text or cast: 36
- pinned in the suite: ROWS: HELD TOOLKIT-JSONB-TYPE-EXACT-EVERYWHERE-RULING-1: ne/nin read a value of another JSON type through its cast: 8
- pinned in the suite: ROWS: HELD TOOLKIT-JSONB-TYPE-EXACT-EVERYWHERE-RULING-1: under the default equality=extraction every value operator reads a value of another JSON type through its text or cast ("5" is 5, "true" is true): 8
- pinned in the suite: ROWS: KEPT (row 3 documents it): filter= != does not match a row or element without the field: 33
- pinned in the suite: ROWS: KEPT (row 3 documents it): filter= == 5 does not match a stored "5" (type-exact): 6
